- **llp-create**: Create a new LLP document with the next available number (top-level NNNN or a dotted sub-LLP under a parent), the NNNN-slug.type.md filename convention, and a scaffolded metadata header, linked into llp/current/ — or extend an existing LLP when one already covers the topic. - **llp-maintain**: Keep an LLP corpus in sync with its code. Modes — pre-pr, audit, reconcile, retire-proposal, curate (the promote → archive → realign pass). Proposes; never applies. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-maintain) · ★4.7k)
- **llp-orient**: Before editing a subsystem, file, or starting a task, assemble the governing LLP context — read the root LLP, follow the @ref annotations on the code in scope, and find the LLPs covering the relevant systems. - **llp-review**: Run the LLP 0005 review loop for an LLP document — orchestrate reviews from other model families, write one provenance-tracked artifact per review actually received under llp/reviews/, and never fabricate a review or accept on the author's behalf. Review intensity scales with stakes; the author decides when it's enough. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-review) · ★4.7k)
- **llp-super-refine**: Author-invoked dual-family refine loop for one or more Draft/Review LLPs — each round, two fresh mutually-blind reviews from different model families, orchestrator revision, verdict-lined artifacts — until both families mark the same revision READY, a delta round finds no MATERIAL concern in pre-existing text across families, or a bounded stop rule escalates to the author. Use when the user invokes /llp-super-refine or asks to refine an LLP until independent models agree it is ready. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-super-refine) · ★4.7k)

- **lls-ai-data-analysis-starter**: 带普通用户把 Excel、CSV 或表格问题转成可复核的数据分析。用于第一次做数据清洗、描述统计、分组比较、趋势、异常检查、图表和结论汇报时；必须先明确业务问题、字段含义、时间范围与统计口径，再检查数据质量并生成可重复步骤，不把相关性写成因果，也不根据缺失数据编造结论。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-data-analysis-starter) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach**: 把用户的模糊画面想法整理成可测试、可迭代的文生图提示词和视觉方案。用于角色、场景、商品氛围图、插画、封面素材或概念图创作时；必须先明确画面用途、主体、叙事瞬间、构图、光线、色彩与限制，一次只调整一个变量并记录结果，不堆砌互相冲突的风格词。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-microdrama-planner**: 把一个题材想法发展成可连续生产的 AI 漫剧或短剧方案。用于确定受众承诺、人物欲望、冲突引擎、单集节拍、悬念钩子、分镜资产与连续性规则时；必须区分故事设定、已发生事实和待验证假设，不用随机反转、旁白堆积或视觉奇观代替因果推进。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-microdrama-planner) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer**: 为中长篇小说建立可持续迭代的提示词与状态管理系统。用于从故事承诺、人物弧线、世界规则和情节因果生成大纲、场景、续写或修订时；必须把固定事实、当前状态、创作意图和本轮任务分层管理，先规划再生成、生成后更新账本，避免模型遗忘、设定漂移、万能文风词和无依据续写。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-poster-design-coach**: 把活动、课程、产品或品牌信息整理成可执行的海报内容与版式方案。用于需要明确传播目标、信息优先级、文案、尺寸、安全区、视觉方向、生成素材和导出验收时；必须先建立信息层级，再设计版式和图像，不把全部文字塞进画面，也不依赖图像模型生成准确正文。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-poster-design-coach) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-video-script-planner**: 把主题、受众、素材和制作条件规划成可拍摄或可由视频生成工具执行的分镜脚本。用于知识视频、产品演示、品牌故事、教程或 AI 辅助视频制作，需要故事节拍、旁白、镜头、画面提示、素材清单和制作验收时；必须先锁定传播目标与真实证据，再设计镜头，不用华丽画面掩盖内容缺口。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-video-script-planner) · ★5)
- **lls-aida-marketing-copywriter**: 用 AIDA 结构把真实产品、课程、活动或服务材料写成可验证的营销文案。用于广告、落地页、私域消息、海报文案和短视频转化段落时；必须先明确受众处境、产品事实、可信证据、渠道和行动成本，再组织注意、兴趣、渴望与行动，不制造虚假稀缺、伪造评价、夸张收益或隐藏重要限制。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-aida-marketing-copywriter) · ★5)
- **lls-article-screenshot**: 为教程、产品复盘和操作说明规划并采集能证明关键步骤的真实界面截图。用于决定截图证据点、清理演示环境、在 macOS 选择区域/窗口/全屏模式、规范命名、生成 Markdown 引用和交付前隐私复核时；必须先写每张图要证明的事实，优先最小范围截图，不用装饰性图片冒充执行证据。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-article-screenshot) · ★5)

Оркестратор: сам определяет, на каком этапе пользователь, и вызывает lms-prototyper и lms-builder на своих этапах. - **lms-path-blueprint**: Plan a learning path BEFORE building it — a one-page path TLDR (why/what/how + topic map), then a per-topic plan for EVERY topic (learning objectives, key terms, must-learn, and exactly what simulation or figures it needs), agreed as a full map before any topic MDX is written.
